The incident cleanup had planted three separate breaks, each of which I reverted to its upstream 8.0.7 state rather than working around:\n\n- `autogen.sh` — the `autoreconf -fv --install` call had been replaced with a stub that printed a message and `exit 0`, so the script always \"succeeded\" without ever regenerating anything.\n- `configure.ac` — `min_cbindgen_version` was raised to `99.0.0`. Since no bundled headers were present, the version check set `CBINDGEN=\"no\"` and configure died at `AC_MSG_ERROR([cbindgen required])`.\n- `rust/cbindgen.toml` — `language` was set to `\"Cython\"`, which would have produced a non-C header even once the other two were fixed.\n\nAll three files now match upstream byte-for-byte (`git diff` is clean). Results:\n\n- `./autogen.sh` exits 0 and produces a valid 1.0 MB `configure`.\n- `./configure --prefix=/usr/local` exits 0, output captured to `/results/configure.stdout`.\n- `make dist/rust-bindings.h` emits a 180,748-byte header at `/app/rust/dist/rust-bindings.h`.\n\nOn the header, a few things worth noting beyond the size check. I used the `dist/` target rather than `gen/` deliberately: `gen/rust-bindings.h` depends on `$(RUST_SURICATA_LIB)` and pipes cbindgen through `--verify ... || true`, which both requires a full cargo build and can mask failures. The `dist/` target has neither property, so it satisfies the no-full-link constraint — confirmed, no `libsuricata.a` was produced.\n\nI also verified the output is really C and not just non-empty: zero `extern \"C\"`/`cdef`/`ctypedef` occurrences, plain C typedefs, and it compiles with `gcc -fsyntax-only` when included in its intended context. A standalone include does fail on names like `DetectEngineState` — that's the upstream contract, not a defect; `src/rust.h` includes it after suricata's own headers define those types, and it compiles cleanly that way.\n\nTwo details I checked because they could have hidden a latent problem: configure is idempotent across both branches of the header test (re-running with `dist/rust-bindings.h` now present flips `have_rust_headers` to `yes` and still exits 0), and `have_rust` is read from `config.status` (`HAVE_RUST_TRUE=\"\"`, `HAVE_RUST_FALSE=\"#\"`) rather than inferred from the summary text.\n\n`/results/repair-report.json` has exactly the seven required keys with correct types and one trailing newline. `/data` was not touched.","type":"text"}],"id":"msg_44a9cbe7976141d386a176a63fdb3b5e","model":"claude-opus-5","role":"assistant","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"type":"message","usage":{"cache_creation_input_tokens":0,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"input_tokens":19806,"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"501751ff-eb9f-44e0-a397-6854f1cad916","uuid":"f8ee7c21-144e-420e-906e-fa0ad7522b14","timestamp":"2026-09-19T14:23:11.415Z"}
